Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) is emerging as a promising alternative to reinforcement learning for LLM post-training, yet its effectiveness in multilingual settings remains underexplored. We study OPD and its advanced variant, On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$), for mathematical reasoning in English, Korean, and Japanese. OPD$^2$ improves OPD by using the probability gap between a post-trained teacher and its base model as the learning signal. Experiments with Qwen3 show that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms the original OPD, with particularly strong improvements in Korean and Japanese, and generally narrows the English-Korean performance gap. We further find that English-only OPD can also increase performance for Korean and Japanese, but often shifts the responses toward English, highlighting the importance of multilingual data to preserving target-language responses.

</details>


### [147] [Hierarchical Latent Prediction for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05806)
*Chang Shi,Tim Pearce,Manan Tomar,Siddhartha Sen,John Langford*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HiLP的层次化潜变量预测方法，通过引入更高层次的抽象潜变量，改进长时序推理与规划中的误差累积问题，并在编码和多步推理任务中取得了更好的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NTP等语言模型训练方法在应对长时序推理和规划时存在不足，辅助任务如MTP和NextLat虽然有所改进，但存在预测视野有限或多步展开误差累积等问题，亟需更高效的辅助目标。

Method: 提出了Hierarchical Latent Prediction (HiLP) 方法，在模型中引入高层抽象潜变量作为辅助预测目标，通过在潜空间进行更有效的长距离预测，减少因多步抽样带来的误差扩散。

Result: 实验显示HiLP模型能够生成更具连贯性的长时序 belief state 表达，并在编码和多步推理测试中优于传统方法，同时提升推理过程的解码效率。

Conclusion: HiLP方法有效缓解了推理过程中潜空间多步展开的误差累积问题，促进了长距离推理和规划能力的提升，对于多步推理和高效解码具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: While standard Next-Token Prediction (NTP) lays the foundation of language model pre- training, its teacher-forced training paradigm may not be optimal for long-horizon reasoning and planning. Recent works such as Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) and Next-Latent prediction (NextLat) try to mitigate the problem through predicting multiple future tokens and self-supervised prediction in the latent space. However, those auxiliary objectives either have a limited horizon or suffer from compounding error from multi-step rollout. We introduce Hierarchical Latent Prediction (HiLP), which introduces an auxiliary higher-level abstract latent to help reduce the error accumulation effect in latent-space rollouts. Experiments show that HiLP can lead to longer-horizon coherent belief state representation and demonstrate the effectiveness of our method across coding and multi-step reasoning benchmarks, and offers more speculative decoding efficiency.

</details>


### [148] [M$^3$R-Bench: A Unified Benchmark for Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Metaphor Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05817)
*Hong Jiang,Junnan Zhu,Jingwang Huang,Xiao Sun,Yuming Yang,Jiang Zhong,Ruirui Chen,Jingman Shi,Hao Wu,Nayu Liu,Xinyi Jiang,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了M3R-Bench和M3R-Reasoner，专注多模态隐喻理解，并实现了目前最好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态隐喻理解的基准评价主要依赖孤立子任务，缺乏结合视觉和文本证据的推理解释，难以准确评估模型是否基于多模态信息建立了合理的隐喻映射。

Method: 作者构建了包含1000个人工标注样本的M3R-Bench数据集，联合标注隐喻出现、目标-源映射、情感及分阶段推理，并提出基于课程学习和任务感知强化学习的M3R-Reasoner模型，提升对多模态隐喻的理解与解释能力。

Result: 实验发现，现有模型往往忽视视觉证据、过度依赖文本表面线索，导致隐喻理解不准确。M3R-Reasoner在仅8B参数下，在四项统一任务指标上，超越了更大规模的专有多模态大模型（MLLM），并在视觉证据和情感解释方面比分别领先GPT-5.5及Claude-Sonnet-4.6大幅提升。

Conclusion: M3R-Bench和M3R-Reasoner有效弥补了当前多模态隐喻理解评价和解释的不足，为模型合理使用多模态信息建立隐喻推理树立了新标杆。

Abstract: Metaphor enables the understanding of abstract concepts through cross-domain mappings while conveying affective attitudes. In multimodal scenarios, visual and textual information jointly construct Target--Source mappings, requiring both conceptual understanding and cross-modal reasoning. However, existing benchmarks mainly evaluate metaphor understanding through isolated subtasks and lack evidence-grounded explanations, making it difficult to assess whether models establish mappings grounded in visual and textual cues.To address these limitations, we introduce M$^3$R-Bench, a unified and evidence-grounded benchmark containing 1,000 image--text instances with human-verified annotations. Guided by Conceptual Metaphor Theory and theories of nonliteral language understanding, M$^3$R-Bench provides joint annotations for metaphor occurrence, Target--Source mapping, sentiment, and stage-wise explanations following ``evidence identification--mapping establishment--sentiment inference.''Evaluations on M$^3$R-Bench reveal that existing models often overlook visual evidence, rely on superficial textual cues, and produce inaccurate Target--Source mappings, exposing a cross-modal evidence--mapping mismatch. To address this mismatch, we propose M$^3$R-Reasoner, which combines curriculum-based reasoning supervision with task-aware reinforcement learning to align model reasoning with metaphor interpretation. Experiments show that, with only an 8B-parameter backbone, M$^3$R-Reasoner outperforms larger proprietary MLLMs across four unified-task metrics and improves Visual Evidence and Sentiment Justification scores over GPT-5.5 by 28.45 and 30.11 points, respectively, while surpassing Claude-Sonnet-4.6 by 8.00 points in mean rubric score. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/hongshi4/M3R-Bench.

</details>


### [149] [Decomposed Entailment for Factuality Checking and Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05823)
*Achir Oukelmoun,Nasredine Semmar,Gaël De Chalendar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、无需参考、黑盒化的LLM幻觉检测框架HallDetect，能在多个生成任务下有效检测事实错误，并在主流基线中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型在生成任务中容易出现事实错误（幻觉），严重影响其可靠性。现有检测手段局限于摘要任务或需大量资源，缺乏通用、高效的解决方案。

Method: HallDetect采用分解式事实性评估，将模型生成内容分解为原子事实，每一项由紧凑的编码器推断模型借助多层次的对比源片段进行核查，通过非对称聚合分数，单一明显矛盾即可判为幻觉。该系统无需引用原始上下文，端到端黑盒运行并输出每个错误的定位审核路径。

Result: 在标准化实验协议下，采用等量算力（4-bit量化、消费级硬件）与生成式及嵌入式基线比较，HallDetect在四个测试基准中三项取得更好表现，并在不同主干模型下表现稳定。

Conclusion: HallDetect是通用高效的LLM幻觉检测工具，不仅检测性能优越，还可追踪错误源，为模型可靠性提升提供了切实可行的方案。

Abstract: The reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs) is often compromised by factual inconsistencies, including hallucinations---cases where generated content is not supported by the underlying source. We present HallDetect, a lightweight, reference-free, and black-box framework for hallucination detection that we evaluate not only on summarization but across a broader range of source-grounded generation settings. HallDetect builds on decomposition-based factuality evaluation: generated content is decomposed into atomic claims, each verified by a compact encoder-based entailment model through a contrastive formulation over a multi-scale library of source chunks, and aggregated with an asymmetric score in which a single confidently contradicted claim flags the response. Under a controlled protocol in which all methods share the same 4-bit quantized backbones and consumer-grade hardware budget, HallDetect outperforms comparably resourced generative and embedding-based baselines on three of four benchmarks while remaining stable across backbone families, and yields a claim-to-span audit trail that localizes each error.

</details>


### [150] [MoCA: Implicit Social Context Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05825)
*Wenhao Xu,Kaiwen Zhang,Hao Li,Maowei You,Yongzheng Ji,Siyuan Zuo,Jingxuan Yu,Sina A,Xinyao Tan,Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MoCA任务和数据集，并用CoDAR框架显著提升了模型对隐性社会情境的理解能力，但与人类推理还有明显差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中，人类社交通过隐性信号传递感情和意图，而这些隐性社会情境缺乏系统性、形式化的研究方法。这导致当前大模型难以理解和处理这类复杂的、间接的信息表达。

Method: 提出了MoCA（Implicit Social Context Analysis）任务，并构建了3,108条多模态真实数据集，细致标注了表达者、对象、情感、意图等认知信息。针对主流大模型难以理解隐性社交情况的问题，作者提出了CoDAR（一种基于认知冲突的归纳推理框架），通过建模表达和真实预期之间的差异，推断隐藏的心理状态。

Result: 实验表明，现有多模态大语言模型在MoCA任务上表现较差。采用CoDAR后，模型性能有了显著提升，但距离人类水平依然有明显差距。

Conclusion: MoCA数据集和CoDAR方法为隐性社会理解提供了新方向，突破了以往仅依赖显性线索的局限，但尚未弥补人机推理之间的根本差距，未来还有提升空间。

Abstract: Human social communication, such as affection and intent, is often conveyed in highly implicit ways, where underlying meanings are expressed through indirect, socially and culturally grounded signals rather than explicit statements. Such implicit social contexts are pervasive in real-world interactions, yet there remains a lack of a formal and systematic framework for studying them. In this paper, we introduce Implicit Social Context Analysis (MoCA), a novel task that systematically models implicit social scenarios along three key dimensions: affection, intent, and stance. We construct a high-quality benchmark containing 3,108 multimodal instances collected from real-world sources, with fine-grained cognitive annotations revealing who expresses what toward whom, as well as how and why it is conveyed. Using the MoCA dataset, we show that state-of-the-art multimodal large language models struggle significantly with this task because of their reliance on explicit cues and limited ability to reason over latent social contexts. To address this challenge, we propose Conflict-Driven Abductive Reasoning (CoDAR), a novel framework that models the discrepancy between observed expressions and expected truthful behavior as cognitive conflict, thereby enabling the inference of hidden mental states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoDAR substantially improves model performance. Nevertheless, a large gap from human reasoning remains, highlighting the fundamental difficulty of implicit social understanding.

</details>


### [151] [Enhancing Social Intelligence in LLMs with Hierarchical Reasoning and Utterance-Level Goal Rewarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05832)
*Xiaofeng Wang,Kakam Chong,Shuai Xiao,DeXin Kong,Qingyuan Tian,Chen Ju,Xu Yan,Shuai Zhao,Fei Huang,Rui Wang,Shuguang Han,jufeng chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新框架TSR及算法LHRL-VGR，提升大语言模型在多智能体社交谈判任务中的表现，并优于当前GPT-4o基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在结构化任务上表现优异，但在动态的社交交互中由于目标和策略变化快，现有方法通常采用统一的奖励机制，无法贴合每步的实际目标与策略动机，导致效果不佳。

Method: 提出Think-Strategy-Response (TSR) 框架，将社交对话分为高层次策略规划与低层次语言执行；为优化该框架，设计了线性分层强化学习加方差门控奖励（LHRL-VGR）算法，根据目标达成情况的方差动态分配奖励，权衡目标完成和策略遵循。

Result: 在SOTOPIA基准上，所提出方法微调的Qwen2.5-7B智能体在目标完成率上较GPT-4o提升7.32%，达到当前最佳水平。

Conclusion: 通过分解对话结构与引入动态激励机制，可显著提升大模型在复杂、多智能体社交场景中的目标达成能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel in structured tasks but struggle with dynamic social interactions, where success requires long-term goal coordination and rapid adaptation. Current methods often apply uniform goal-based rewards to every utterance, overlooking the specificity of objectives at each dialogue turn and failing to account for the rationale of potential strategies. Inspired by the Theory of Planned Behavior, we propose the Think-Strategy-Response (TSR) framework, which decomposes social dialogue into two hierarchical stages: high-level strategic planning and low-level linguistic execution. To optimize TSR, we introduce Linearized Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning with Variance-Gated Rewards (LHRL-VGR), a novel algorithm that dynamically routes rewards - balancing goal completion and strategy adherence - based on the variance of goal achievement scores. Experiments on the SOTOPIA benchmark show that our approach fine-tunes a Qwen2.5-7B agent to surpass the GPT-4o baseline by 7.32% in goal completion success, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance in multi-agent social negotiation tasks.

</details>


### [152] [MameLoshnLM: Yiddish Language Model and Evaluation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05850)
*Uri Katz,Omer Goldman,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Reut Tsarfaty,Noah A. Smith*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了MameLoshnLM，这是第一个专为意第绪语设计的开源8B参数语言模型，并伴随高质量语料库与评测基准。模型在多任务评测中优于同体量的开源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意第绪语虽然历史文本丰富，但数字化资源稀缺，尤其是高质量语料和有效评测基准的缺乏，限制了其NLP发展。现有多语种模型中的意第绪语文本往往质量较差，存在误分类、机器翻译等问题。

Method: 作者开发了Oytser高质量意第绪语训练语料库，结合了现代网络材料与文学文本，并设计了Kashes多任务基准（含翻译、语言分析、信息抽取和理解）。在此基础上，对Llama 3.1 8B继续预训练，得到MameLoshnLM。

Result: MameLoshnLM在Kashes多任务基准上的表现超过了同规模的开源对比模型，并且在词汇和形态等语言特征上优于通用多语种模型。

Conclusion: MameLoshnLM为意第绪语NLP打下了基础，也为其他历史悠久但数字化程度低的语言开发高质量大模型提供了可行模板。

Abstract: We present MameLoshnLM, the first open-source 8B-parameter language model built specifically for Yiddish. Despite Yiddish's rich textual tradition, its limited digital presence and the scarcity of reliable evaluation resources have constrained progress in Yiddish language modeling. Existing multilingual corpora and benchmarks are often poor proxies for the language, containing substantial amounts of noisy, machine-translated, and misclassified text. We address these gaps by introducing Oytser, a high-quality Yiddish pretraining corpus that combines contemporary web-native sources with literary materials, and Kashes, a multi-task benchmark spanning translation, linguistic analysis, information extraction, and language understanding. Using these resources, we continue pretraining Llama 3.1 8B to obtain MameLoshnLM. Across the tasks in the benchmark, MameLoshnLM outperforms open baselines of similar scale. Our analyses show that these gains are not only quantitative: relative to general-purpose multilingual models, MameLoshnLM better captures language-defining lexical and morphological patterns, pointing to a broader failure mode of noisy web-scale multilingual data for low-resource languages. Our results provide both a foundation for Yiddish NLP and a practical template for language model development in historically rich but digitally underrepresented languages.

</details>


### [153] [Mapping Similarity Spaces across Embedding Models with Synthetic Query Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05857)
*Marcin Rozmus,Peter van der Putten*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为Synthetic Query Probing（合成查询探测）的方法，用于解决不同嵌入模型间相似度分数不可比的问题，并通过学习分数映射函数提高了相似度阈值的迁移与重用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同的嵌入模型（如用于检索-增强生成系统的embedding models）由于内部几何属性差异，使得相似度分数在各模型间不可直接比较，造成模型迁移困难，也使相似度阈值难以复用。因此，找到上述分数关系的统一或映射机制，成为实际应用中的亟需解决的问题。

Method: 作者提出通过生成查询（synthetic queries）与文档配对（query-chunk pairs），无需参考文献下大规模分析各模型的相似度分数行为。具体采用线性映射、单调回归（isotonic）、分位数映射等方法学习分数分布之间的映射函数，并在SciFact及一个私有数据集上进行实验评估。

Result: 实验表明，不同模型虽然在排名结果上较为一致，但分数本身存在系统性偏离。通过学习得到的映射函数，特别是使用isotonic regression，可以部分弥合不同分数空间，提高阈值的迁移性与可用性。

Conclusion: 本研究指出不同embedding模型之间需要校准，同时提出的Synthetic Query Probing可作为大规模分析嵌入模型相似度可比性的有效框架。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems rely on similarity scores to retrieve relevant content, yet scores are not directly comparable across embedding models due to differing geometric properties, complicating model migration and limiting threshold reuse. We study how similarity scores can be related by learning mappings between score distributions rather than embeddings. We introduce Synthetic Query Probing, generating queries from documents to create controlled query-chunk pairs, enabling large-scale, reference-free analysis of cross-model similarity behavior. We evaluate the approach on multiple embedding configurations and learn score conversion functions using linear, isotonic, and quantile mappings. Experiments on SciFact and a proprietary corpus show that while models largely agree on rankings, their absolute scores exhibit systematic distortions. Learned mappings partially align these spaces and improve threshold portability, with isotonic regression performing best. Our results highlight the need for cross-model calibration and position Synthetic Query Probing as a scalable framework for analyzing embedding comparability.

</details>


### [154] [MACRO: Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05872)
*Paweł Batorski,Abtin Pourhadi,Akylgali Aitaza,Przemysław Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种不修改大模型参数的动态路由方法MACRO，实现了更高性能与大幅度加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型（LLM）按层顺序执行，动态层路由有潜力提升表现，但现有方法常需微调模型、推理时耗时的搜索或依赖真值标签，实际应用受限。

Method: 提出Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers (MACRO) 框架，将每层路由建模为依赖多种上下文信息的马尔可夫策略。它支持跳过、重复和残差状态操作，基于训练反馈优化路由分布，并用Viterbi算法高效推理最优路径，过程中无需修改模型本身参数。

Result: 在多个公开权重的LLM上进行评测，MACRO平均提升+5%准确率，尤其小模型收益最大。相较于当前最优方法Dr. LLM，性能高7.2%，搜索路由时间减少9.4倍（14.8小时降至1.6小时）。

Conclusion: MACRO无需模型微调，带来显著性能提升和效率加速，展示了动态层路由在LLM上的落地潜力。

Abstract: Standard Large Language Models (LLMs) execute layers sequentially. Dynamic layer routing, i.e. search for a different execution path through layers involving layer repetitions, skips and other moves, can improve performance. Existing routing approaches often require updating model weights, running expensive search loops per test instance, or demand ground-truth labels during inference. In this work, we propose Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers (MACRO), a framework that learns task-specific routes over LLM architectures without modifying underlying parameters. MACRO models layer routing as a context-dependent Markov policy conditioned on layer indices, computation budget phases, directional displacements, and operator context, supporting skip, repeat, and residual hidden-state addition operations. The Markov route distribution is updated via feedback on training data and decoded using a top-k Viterbi algorithm to isolate high-probability candidate programs. We evaluate MACRO across diverse reasoning and knowledge benchmarks on multiple open-weight LLMs. MACRO achieves a +5.0% average accuracy improvement over the unrouted baselines, with largest gains on small models. We outperform the best dynamic routing approach Dr. LLM by +7.2%, while reducing route-search time 9.4x (from 14.8 to 1.6 hours). Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Batorskq/MACRO.

</details>


### [155] [Causal Episodic Memory for Feedback-Driven Agent Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05906)
*Khang Nhat Hoang Vo,Tam Minh Chu,Anh Trac Duc Dinh,Thuyen Vinh Ha Bui,Tho Quan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练即可利用以往修正经验提升Text-to-SQL能力的方法MERIT，并在Spider和BIRD数据集上验证其提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大模型修错时丢弃成功经验导致后续修复效率低、易重复探索的问题，探索是否可通过利用先前最终修正结果提升后续修复能力，而无需再次训练模型。

Method: 提出MERIT代理，核心是维护一个即时、双极性记忆（成功修正与失败探索），使用确定性分类器为失败类型分类，再据此利用混合检索器（词法+稠密），在oracle协助下，仅允许从早期最终修正记忆检索作为参考，在每次修订生成前为冻结模型提供检索到的信息。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型上评估，MERIT在Spider数据集上将执行准确率从66.34%提升到69.79%，BIRD数据集从47.35%提升到48.44%。在Spider上提升较为显著，BIRD提升较小。与未分类的动态检索方式表现接近，但推理开销远低于Reflexion-style记忆。消融实验还分析了负面记忆、类型条件、混合检索方法及经验的价值。

Conclusion: 基于跨查询的因果性记忆在特定条件下确实能提升修复性能，但在部分数据集及场景中，使用更大范围的记忆表示可能更有效，需依据具体任务权衡使用方式。

Abstract: LLM agents that repair failures often discard successful corrections, forcing later episodes to rediscover similar solutions. We study whether finalized repair outcomes can improve subsequent Text-to-SQL episodes without parameter updates. We introduce MERIT, a training-free agent that maintains an online dual-polarity memory of oracle-verified corrections and observed unsuccessful directions. Under oracle-assisted benchmark feedback, only memories from earlier finalized episodes are eligible for retrieval. A deterministic classifier assigns a coarse failure type, which conditions a hybrid lexical-dense retriever before the frozen model generates each revision. Using Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct with identical initial predictions and repair budgets, MERIT improves execution accuracy over stateless iterative repair from \(66.34\%\) to \(69.79\%\) on Spider and from \(47.35\%\) to \(48.44\%\) on BIRD. Paired analyses provide clear evidence for the Spider gain but weaker evidence on BIRD. MERIT is not reliably separated from untyped dynamic retrieval on either benchmark, while Reflexion-style memory reaches \(51.24\%\) on BIRD at substantially higher inference cost. Ablations show that negative memory contributes modestly, the value of type conditioning and lexical--dense ranking is dataset dependent, and schema-local experience provides the most consistent benefit. These results clarify when causal cross-query memory improves repair and when broader memory representations remain preferable.

</details>


### [156] [Clinical Communication Processing with Models Trained on LLM-Generated Synthetic Data: A Structured Survey and Novel Application Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05993)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大型语言模型生成合成医疗沟通语料来推动医疗自然语言处理（NLP）系统的发展，尤其是在缺乏真实标注数据的场景中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗沟通涉及丰富的语境和角色信息，这些重要信息通常不在结构化表格数据中体现，而在临床交流（如病患描述、护理交接等）中频繁出现。由于真实医疗语料私密、分散且标注成本高，因此现有良好标注的沟通语料极其稀缺，制约了医疗NLP的发展。

Method: 作者系统梳理了基于不同源信息（如病历、症状列表等）、交流形式和参与方，用大型语言模型自动合成医疗沟通语料的方法，并通过十三个案例覆盖多种低标签或无标签沟通场景（如EMS报告、护士交接等），探索利用人工合成沟通数据训练NLP系统。

Result: 合成沟通语料可以激发临床NLP系统的构建与应用。微调的编码器模型在多个任务中表现优于零样本大模型基线。合成语料中包含有意降质的样本有助于提升系统鲁棒性。最大限制在于大多数评估仅在合成数据上，真实数据验证还较缺乏。

Conclusion: 人工合成的医疗沟通语料正逐步成为实用研究资源，未来实现大规模可复用的医学沟通基础设施需加强真实数据转化、安全性和外部有效性验证。

Abstract: Much clinical value is conveyed not through structured records but through communication: exchanges in which patients describe symptoms, clinicians reason and give instructions, ambulances hand over to emergency departments, and nurses pass on a shift. Such language differs from tabular data because meaning depends on speaker role, intent, causality, uncertainty, omission, and channel noise. Healthcare natural language processing must therefore interpret information as conveyed rather than coded. This requires well-annotated corpora, which are scarce because authentic exchanges are private, fragmented, and costly to annotate. Large language models offer a way forward by transforming clinical sources, such as records, diagnostic labels, symptom lists, or care plans, into written and transcribed communication for downstream models. We present a structured narrative survey organized by source representation, communication form and participants, generation method, and downstream task, complemented by thirteen novel case studies. These build clinical NLP systems for communication channels and languages without labeled real-world data, including EMS pre-arrival reports, field-radio casualty documentation, nurse handoffs, patient-portal triage, and low-resource discharge communication. They show that synthetic communication can bootstrap such systems. Findings include the competitiveness of fine-tuned encoder models over evaluated zero-shot baselines and the value of deliberately degraded communication for robustness. The main limitation is that most studies evaluate on held-out synthetic communication, while train-on-synthetic, test-on-authentic evidence remains limited. We conclude that syn-thetic clinical communication is becoming a practical research resource; establishing it as reusable clinical infrastructure will require authentic-data transfer, safety and external validation.

</details>


### [157] [EpiBench: Can LLMs Understand Epitopes for Antibody Drug Discovery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06022)
*Zirui Wang,Jiaqi Wang,Qinghan Wang,Yuzhi Xu,Gang Du,Tingjun Hou,Odin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了EpiBench，这是一个用于评估大语言模型（LLMs）表位推理能力的基准，用于推动抗体药物发现中的模型进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前LLMs在生物医学领域表现优秀，但它们是否能仅凭序列推断抗原上抗体结合的表位尚不明确。现有表位资源局限于单一任务或需求结构信息，且缺乏覆盖抗体开发流程关键决策的系统化评估。因此，需要一个能自动评分、聚焦序列推理能力的诊断基准。

Method: 作者开发了EpiBench基准，包含1609份基于结构数据、功能实验和深度突变逃逸测量精心整理的样本，涵盖五类相关任务（目标区发现、抗体条件下的表位识别、表位分箱、功能表位评估、抗体逃逸评估），支持自动化、无结构、无知识库查询的序列推理评测。对九种通用LLM进行了系统性基准测试，并通过多维度分析其表现和不足。

Result: 当前LLM能够部分捕捉表位信息，但在抗体序列细致推理、长距离残基定位以及生物逻辑推理上存在明显局限，难以满足实际抗体设计需求。

Conclusion: EpiBench为评测和推动序列感知的生物医学LLM能力提供了首个自动化诊断工具，有助于今后更可靠LLM辅助抗体发现研究。

Abstract: Epitopes determine where antibodies bind antigens and shape downstream therapeutic properties such as functional blockade and escape resistance, making epitope understanding central to antibody drug discovery. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong biomedical reasoning ability, it remains unclear whether they can infer epitope information directly from antigen and antibody sequences. Existing epitope resources typically focus on isolated prediction tasks or rely on specialized structural settings, while general protein benchmarks do not evaluate epitope-centered decisions across the antibody development workflow. To address this gap, we introduce EpiBench, a closed-book, sequence-based, and automatically scorable benchmark for evaluating epitope reasoning in LLMs. EpiBench contains 1,609 curated samples grounded in structural antibody--antigen contacts, curated functional B-cell assays, and deep mutational scanning escape measurements. It covers five connected tasks: targetable region discovery, antibody-conditioned epitope identification, epitope binning, functional epitope assessment, and antibody escape assessment, with controlled sampling to reduce shortcut-based evaluation artifacts. We evaluate nine general-purpose LLMs and analyze their behavior through task-specific baselines, antigen length stratification, explicit-reasoning comparison, and failure-mode inspection. The results show that current LLMs capture partial epitope-related signals but remain limited in antibody-specific sequence grounding, long-context residue localization, and biologically grounded reasoning. Therefore, EpiBench provides a diagnostic testbed for measuring and improving sequence-aware biomedical LLMs toward reliable LLM-assisted antibody discovery.

</details>


### [158] [FormBharo: Designing and Evaluating a Voice Agent for Conversational Form Filling in Rural India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06027)
*Aman Dalmia,Sanskriti Midha,Jigar Doshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出并部署了一个名为FormBharo的语音助手，用于帮助印度文盲或半文盲群体通过电话完成政府社保表单的填写，并公布了评测基准数据集和效果分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在印度，社会福利普及高度依赖表格填写，但大部分弱势群体缺乏读写能力，传统依赖基层健康工作者手动逐个登记，效率低、资源浪费严重，因此开发自动化语音表单填写工具可以释放宝贵人力，提高登记与服务覆盖效率。

Method: 构建了FormBharo系统，结合大语言模型（LLMs）与确定性规则校验及流程控制，通过电话与用户口语交流，自动填写结构化表单。项目与印度著名NGO合作，针对低收入、讲印地语的新生母亲健康登记场景进行试点，并公开了FormVoiceAgentBench基准数据集，涵盖多轮对话和真实人声录音，以评测语音识别、信息抽取和回复生成等子任务及整体表单完成表现。

Result: 当LLM处理真实噪声语音转写文本时，端到端表单完成率最高下降约41个百分点。引入规则控制，可以修正大量抽取错误，使小型廉价模型在最终表单完成效果上媲美甚至超过大模型。各组件单项表现无法准确预测整体效果，最佳模型方案需通过整体测试确定。除准确率外，成本和延迟也是方案选择的关键维度，采用Pareto权衡方法综合选型。

Conclusion: FormBharo是面向印度弱势群体、可电话完成表单填写的首个语音智能试点应用。事实证明，综合采用大模型与规则机制能在成本、效率和服务覆盖之间取得较好平衡。本工作推动了语音智能在实际社会服务中的落地和评测基准建设，有广泛现实意义和推广前景。

Abstract: In India, almost every social benefit starts with a form, yet the people who need these benefits most are often unable to read or write. Reaching them requires a spoken conversation. Today that work falls to frontline health workers who enroll beneficiaries one at a time, a poor use of stretched capacity. We built FormBharo ("fill the form" in Hindi), a voice agent that fills a structured form over a phone call under tight latency and cost budgets by pairing Large Language Models (LLMs) with deterministic, rule-based validation and flow control. It is being piloted with ARMMAN, an NGO running large-scale maternal and child mobile-health programs in India, to enroll low-income, Hindi-speaking mothers in antenatal and postnatal care. To our knowledge, it is the first voice agent piloted to fill an enrollment form for this population. We openly release FormVoiceAgentBench, a benchmark pairing human-recorded Hindi audio with 3,760 multi-turn conversation tests across 960 simulated calls, to evaluate our agent's components (transcription, extraction, reply generation) and end-to-end form completion under real acoustic variations. Form completion drops by up to ~41 points when LLMs receive error-prone real-speech transcripts instead of reference ones. The rule-based controls recover many turn-level extraction errors, helping smaller, cheaper models match or surpass frontier models on form completion. Component performance does not predict end-to-end performance: GPT-5.5 leads turn-level extraction accuracy on reference transcripts (99.8%) but ranks lower on form completion. Since errors both propagate and cancel across the pipeline, the optimal model choice of models emerges only through end-to-end evaluation. Finally, no single model is best across accuracy, cost, and latency at once, so we use a Pareto-based weighted-sum scalarization to select a deployable configuration balancing the three.

</details>


### [159] [Training-Free Token-Level Steering for LLM Personalized Co-Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06069)
*Wenhao Mao,Chengbin Hou,Weixiao Wang,Jialiang Zhu,Min Liu,Yibin Hao,Hairong Lv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SteerWrite的无需训练的个性化协同写作框架，可以高效适配专业领域并显著减少人工编辑量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在个性化应用中缺乏专业领域知识，现有方法（如微调、RAG）存在计算成本高、更新慢或个性化粒度有限等问题，且协同写作领域未得到充分开发。

Method: 提出了SteerWrite框架，无需对基座模型进行梯度更新，针对小数据集做了特殊设计，实现基座模型对专业领域的高效适配，且无需训练过程。

Result: SteerWrite在多个数据集、评测指标和模型上都达到了SOTA，显著降低了人工编辑工作量。

Conclusion: SteerWrite为个性化协同写作提供了高效、无需训练且适用于小数据集的新方法，优于现有方案。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) show great promise for personalization, they often lack specialized domain knowledge. Conventional solutions like fine-tuning struggle with high computational costs and rapid data updates, while Retrieval-Augmented Generation fails to provide fine-grained, token-level steering. Furthermore, chat-based interfaces remain dominant, whereas productive co-writing paradigms have not yet been well exploited beyond the coding domain. To this end, we introduce SteerWrite, a training-free framework designed for personalized co-writing. Our method effectively adapts the base model to specialized domains without gradient updates, with specific designs tailored to small datasets. Experiments demonstrate that SteerWrite achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse datasets, metrics, and models, significantly reducing human editing effort.

</details>


### [160] [Beyond Sequence Order: Syntax-Informed Positional Embeddings for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06111)
*Haris Riaz,Hyungji Kim,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的句法信息位置编码方法SiPE，将句法先验信息融入Transformer模型的位置编码中，在多种任务和基准上取得显著性能提升,且推理成本低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的位置编码忽略了句法结构信息，只考虑了序列中的距离和顺序，而语言理解很多时候依赖于句法信息。因此有必要探索如何将句法结构有效融入Transformer模型。

Method: 提出了SiPE（Syntax-informed Positional Embeddings），通过在预训练阶段引入依存句法分析，学习轻量级句法先验，并兼容三类主流位置编码（绝对、相对、旋转），适用于编码器和解码器。系统性分析了先验信息在模型中的注入方式和位置对性能的影响。

Result: 在GLUE、SyntaxGym等基准任务上，SiPE相比无句法监督的模型，在句法泛化和真实世界语言理解能力上都有明显提升，如SyntaxGym提升10.3%，GLUE提升8.2%，困惑度降低9%。

Conclusion: SiPE能在不增加显著推理成本的同时，提升模型的句法泛化和实际语言理解能力，为句法知识和推理效率之间建立了新的Pareto最优。

Abstract: Positional embeddings (PE) in Transformers encode token distance and order but are largely agnostic to \textit{syntactic structure}. We introduce \textbf{S}yntax-\textbf{i}nformed \textbf{P}ositional \textbf{E}mbeddings (\textbf{SiPE}), which learns a lightweight syntactic prior from dependency parses during pretraining and injects it across all three dominant PE families (absolute, relative, rotary), for both encoders and decoders, leaving self-attention and the rest of the architecture untouched. We isolate \emph{where} and \emph{how} the prior should enter the model, and find it depends on the architecture: for autoregressive decoders that use relative PE, the prior is strongest when coupled multiplicatively with the relative-position term of the attention score, outperforming injection into the input embeddings, into self-attention, or into the positional and attention terms jointly---while for encoders it is best added directly to the input embeddings, composing with each encoder's native positional mechanism. We find that models pre-trained with SiPE improve on the SyntaxGym benchmark by up to $10.3\%$ while simultaneously reducing perplexity by $9.0\%$ over a base model with no syntactic supervision---a metric nearly every existing syntax-injection method instead degrades. Crucially, these gains extend beyond syntactic generalization: SiPE also improves real-world language understanding, raising scores on the GLUE benchmark by up to $8.2\%$ over a model trained without it. Unlike existing syntactic language models that marginalize over many parses at inference or discard syntax at runtime, SiPE conditions on a single parse, establishing a new Pareto frontier between syntactic supervision and inference cost.

</details>


### [161] [Decolonizing Linguistic Policies in Automated Speech Recognition: A Framework for Cross-Culturally Competent Speech AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06141)
*Jay L. Cunningham,Mark Atta Mensah,Richard Martinez,Joao Vieira da Silva Neto,Efi Dawodu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了自动语音识别（ASR）及其在公共服务、医疗和教育等领域中的应用对不同语言群体，特别是低资源、土著和非标准语种的影响，提出了当前技术不仅是工程问题，也是语言政策与社会结构问题。作者提出新的理论工具和框架以提升ASR系统的多样性和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ASR系统对低资源和非主流语言识别效果差，这不仅仅是技术不足，还隐含社会和语言层级的问题。作者希望揭示技术背后的语言政治，并推动ASR领域的公平性与包容性。

Method: 作者综合了语言资本、种族语言意识形态、语言政策和去殖民计算等理论，提出了3M分类（误识别、错位、不信任）和七层多样性模型。文中还提出了参与性框架和最低审核协议，通过将受影响群体参与到ASR系统的设计、评估与治理流程中，提升系统对多样性语言的适应性。

Result: 文章建立了新的概念框架（3M分类和七层模型），并提出了具体的社区参与和审核方法。这些工具有助于识别与评估ASR对弱势群体造成的具体伤害与风险，并提供改善的实践路径。

Conclusion: ASR系统隐含了对语言多样性的偏见，其发展过程应加入受影响群体的视角，共同参与设计和治理，从而推动技术的文化能力和公平性，减少对非主流语言的歧视和伤害。

Abstract: This paper focuses on automatic speech recognition (ASR) and ASR-mediated voice interfaces that shape access to public services, healthcare, and education. We argue that persistent failures for low-resource, Indigenous, and non-standard language varieties are not only technical errors, but also implicit linguistic policies that reproduce colonial language hierarchies. Drawing on linguistic capital, raciolinguistic ideology, language policy research, and decolonial computing, we show how data, metrics, and model priors determine whose voices become machine-legible. We introduce the Three Harms (3M) taxonomy---Misrecognition, Misalignment, and Mistrust---and a seven-layer situatedness model for linguistic diversity in ASR and ASR-mediated voice interfaces. We then propose a participatory framework and minimum audit protocol for culturally competent ASR, positioning affected communities as co-designers, evaluators, and governance partners.

</details>


### [162] [Routing Is Least Learnable Where It Is Most Valuable: Bounds on Representation Routing for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06171)
*Jiaming Wei,Zekun Wu,Adriano Koshiyama,Maria Perez-Ortiz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了Web智能体在不同的观察模式下执行任务的表现，发现采用特定规则或动态选择观察模式对整体性能的提升有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Web智能体通常只能固定采用文本、像素或二者混合的某一种浏览器观察模式，但不同任务、网站或模型组合或许会适合不同的观察模式。作者希望评估如果为每个任务灵活选择最优模式，可以带来多大收益。

Method: 作者在VisualWebArena和WebArena的8组（站点-模型）组合下，比较了6种观察模式，包括：只文本、只像素以及两者混合。通过任务完成率评估，分析模式之间的互补性、失败特点、奖励的真实性，并额外测试了5种动态分配观察模式的路由策略。

Result: 不同观察模式在各类任务中的表现互补，且失败方式各异。理论上为每个任务选最优模式带来的提升很大，但实际受运行噪声影响显著（同一模式重复运行结果12-14%改变），真正可获益有限。仅将未被任何模式解决的任务交给最廉价的模式，可在全部8组测试中节约9.5-30.6%成本，且保持成功率。尝试的5种路由政策没有明显超过选用单一最佳模式。

Conclusion: 动态选择观察模式受限于当前Web智能体表现，瓶颈在于路由所需标签的稀缺性：智能体越弱，触发路由的训练数据越少，在最需优化处难以受益。未来如智能体增强，动态路由策略可能更有发挥空间。论文同时提供了实验中的噪声区间和完整流程。

Abstract: Web agents observe a browser through text, pixels, or both, and the choice is usually fixed once for all tasks. We measure six observation modes across eight site-model combinations (cells) on VisualWebArena and WebArena and ask what choosing per task would buy. The modes are complementary: each solves tasks the others miss, they fail in structurally different ways, and the best choice reverses between task sets. The obvious prize, an oracle that picks a winning mode for every task, looks large but is inflated by run-to-run noise: rerunning the same mode on the same tasks changes 12-14% of outcomes, so a second run of a mode already in hand gains about as much as adding a new one. What survives is a cost bound: sending only the tasks no mode solves to the cheapest mode cuts cost by 9.5-30.6% in 8 of 8 cells at unchanged success. We then test five routing policies (picking the mode, deciding when to spend on the strong mode, a zero-cost rule read off the task text, a confidence cascade, and pooled cost tiers), and none robustly beats simply fixing one well-chosen mode; the one exception is a fragile result in our sparsest cell. The central obstruction is that routing supervision is produced at the agent's success rate: the weaker the agent, the fewer labels a router gets, exactly where routing would be most valuable. This limit belongs to today's agents rather than to routing itself. Label supply and routing opportunity rise together (correlation 0.95 across cells), so a stronger agent can overturn the result, and we report the rerun noise bands and the full measurement protocol.

</details>


### [163] [NeSy-RAG: Neuro-Symbolic RAG for Explainable Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06292)
*Jonas Gann,Michael Gertz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的神经-符号RAG框架NeSy-RAG，将检索文本自动转化为可溯源Prolog代码，提升了问答的可解释性与准确性，在ShARC基准任务中大幅超越了传统RAG方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法虽然能让LLM结合外部知识增强问答表现，但推理过程不透明且难以验证，且缺乏对用户上下文缺失的系统检测，导致输出常出现不完整或错误。

Method: NeSy-RAG首先从检索回来的文本块中提取有语义的命题谓词(转换为布尔表达),随后通过自然语言-代码联合嵌入将谓词检索和组合为Prolog查询。提出了符号知识缺口检测机制，能发现影响查询结果的用户信息缺失，并自动发起补充交互。最终用Prolog执行以获得确定性的答案和清晰的推理轨迹。

Result: 在ShARC基准上，NeSy-RAG在未进行特定领域训练的情况下，实现了61.1%的准确率，显著高于对比组RAG模型的42.8%。

Conclusion: NeSy-RAG不仅实现了问答过程中推理步骤和证据的可溯源与透明，还能检测并补全用户上下文缺失，从而提升了问答输出的质量和准确性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves question answering by grounding large language models (LLMs) in external knowledge such as text corpora. However, its reasoning process remains largely opaque: intermediate reasoning steps are difficult to verify and cannot be reliably attributed to specific evidence. Moreover, missing user-specific context is rarely detected systematically, often leading to incomplete or incorrect output.
  We propose NeSy-RAG, a modular neuro-symbolic RAG framework that synthesizes attributable Prolog modules from retrieved text chunks. For each chunk, the system generates semantically meaningful predicates that encode Boolean claims, which may depend on user facts. Using joint natural language-code embeddings, predicates are retrieved and composed into Prolog queries. To address incomplete user context, we introduce a symbolic knowledge-gap detection mechanism that identifies missing user facts whose truth values affect the query outcome and automatically triggers follow-up interactions.
  Executing the resulting Prolog queries yields deterministic answers together with transparent execution traces that link each reasoning step to its originating source. On the ShARC benchmark, without domain-specific training, NeSy-RAG achieves 61.1% accuracy, outperforming a same-model RAG baseline that achieves 42.8% accuracy.

</details>


### [164] [Benchmarking and Enhancing LLMs for Rule-Intensive Review of National Standard Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06312)
*Tao Wang,Qihao Yang,Rongjiao Liang,Lianghong Lin,Haitao Wang,Xinyu Cao,Tianyong Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GB/T-Bench，这是首个面向中国国家标准文件结构化审核的基准，评估了大语言模型（LLMs）在规则密集型文档审核中的能力，并提出多智能体框架GB/T-Reviewer以提升审核准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在专业任务中表现优异，但在包含复杂规则及结构的国家标准文件审核中的能力尚未评价。人工审核标准文档成本高且难以大规模扩展，现有基准更侧重问答与知识测评，忽视了专业文档的内在质量审核。

Method: 该研究构建了GB/T-Bench基准，设立分层审核分类法，涵盖结构、范围、一致性、规范性等25种可诊断错误类型，并通过规则与LLM结合生成含7306个带追踪性的审核错误样本。评测协议要求对错误位置、审核维度和类型严格匹配，同时使用文档级覆盖率指标。作者还提出GB/T-Reviewer多智能体系统，将审核知识模块化为多项专技能，实现全局与针对性诊断、规则扫描和结果复核。

Result: 在14个主流大语言模型上的实验证明，人-模型之间有明显能力差距，最强模型的审核一致性分数（CMCS）为0.3280，专家为0.6640；而GB/T-Reviewer将最高分提升至0.5094，显示了结构化技能协作框架的价值。

Conclusion: 研究为规则密集型专业文档的AI审核开辟了新方向，GB/T-Bench和GB/T-Reviewer为推动标准化及高风险文档领域可信AI奠定基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly support complex professional tasks, yet their capabilities in rule-intensive document review remain insufficiently evaluated. National standard documents, such as China GB/T standards, offer a representative testbed: they are lengthy, highly structured, and governed by explicit rules for scope, terminology, normative wording, and cross-section consistency. Existing benchmarks focus on domain knowledge and question answering, largely overlooking intrinsic quality review for professional documents. Such reviews rely heavily on human experts, making them costly and difficult to scale. To bridge this gap, we introduce GB/T-Bench, the first benchmark for the structured review of national standard documents. Its GB/T Review Taxonomy is a hierarchical schema covering document structure, scope alignment, normative modality, terminology consistency, and normative references, with 25 diagnosable error types. A controllable counterexample generation mechanism combines deterministic rules and constrained LLM rewriting to process 488 documents into 7,306 traceable review error instances for evaluation. We also develop a diagnosis-oriented evaluation protocol requiring exact matches on error location, review dimension, and error type, plus document-level coverage metrics. We further propose GB/T-Reviewer, a multi-agent framework that converts review knowledge into specialized skills and coordinates global inspection, targeted diagnosis, rule scanning, and result verification. Experiments with 14 mainstream LLMs reveal a substantial human-LLM gap: the strongest model achieves only 0.3280 CMCS versus 0.6640 for experts. GB/T-Reviewer raises the best CMCS to 0.5094, showing the value of structured skill coordination for rule-intensive document review. This work paves the way for trustworthy AI in standardization and other high-stakes document domains.

</details>


### [165] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Benchmarks for Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06329)
*Noam Koren,Roy Bar-Haim,Abigail Goldsteen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需参考基准的框架，利用大语言模型评判员（LLM judges）来全面评估对话智能体任务基准的数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话任务基准的数据质量普遍缺乏有效评测，低质量数据可能导致模型评估结果不可靠，因此需要新方法来系统性分析与提升基准集质量。

Method: 设计了利用大语言模型作为评判员的评测框架，以评估对话基准的数据一致性、复杂度以及策略覆盖率，同时能产出可用于改进的诊断信息。通过与人工标注一致性检验、多种能力的LLM生成的基准及质量扰动实验来验证方法有效性。

Result: 该框架无论在不同领域还是使用不同评判模型情况下，都能较为准确地区分出不同质量等级的对话任务基准。实验也显示评测指标与人工标注有较高一致性。

Conclusion: 文中框架为评估人工和LLM生成的对话智能体任务基准质量提供了实用工具，有助于提升基准整体质量，增强模型评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Task-oriented conversational agents are evaluated using curated or automatically generated benchmarks, yet benchmark quality is rarely assessed. Poor benchmarks may contain inconsistent tasks, simplistic scenarios, or limited policy coverage, leading to unreliable evaluations. We introduce a reference-free framework that uses LLM judges to assess benchmark consistency, complexity, and policy coverage, while providing actionable diagnostics of weaknesses. We validate the framework by demonstrating agreement with independent human annotations and by evaluating benchmarks generated by LLMs of varying capabilities, as well as benchmarks subjected to controlled quality-degrading perturbations. Across domains and judge models, the proposed metrics consistently distinguish between benchmark quality levels. We further demonstrate the framework's applicability to manually curated benchmarks. Our framework offers a practical approach for evaluating synthetic and manually curated conversational-agent benchmarks.

</details>


### [166] [RP-OPSD: Reasoning-Pivot-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation for Multilingual Reasoning Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06347)
*Xinye Wang,Junxiao Liu,Shujian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种新方法RP-OPSD，通过聚焦于推理关键点来提升大语言模型的多语种推理能力，在17种语言的数学推理基准上大幅超越已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语种推理迁移方法（如OPSD）并没有明确关注对多语推理迁移至关重要的推理信号，导致跨语种推理能力受限。作者认为，应将蒸馏重点放在引导推理过程的关键决策点（推理pivot）上。

Method: 提出Reasoning-Pivot-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation（RP-OPSD）方法：通过比较教师有无英语参考解答时的分布变化，将更多蒸馏信号聚焦于推理控制和状态更新等关键token。

Result: 在涵盖17种语言和多难度级别的数学推理基准测试中，RP-OPSD显著优于强多语推理基线和其他OPSD变体。

Conclusion: RP-OPSD能有效提升模型在多语种推理任务的能力，尤其是在关键推理token上的迁移表现，验证了关注推理pivots进行蒸馏的有效性。

Abstract: Multilingual reasoning transfer is crucial for extending reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) beyond high-resource languages. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) and its variants have emerged as a promising paradigm, providing dense token-level supervision on student-generated rollouts, yet their objectives do not explicitly prioritize reasoning signals most critical to cross-lingual transfer. We characterize that target-language reasoning comprises the generation of both surface text and reasoning pivots, which are decisions that advance or redirect the reasoning process and shape subsequent inference. This motivates concentrating privileged distillation around such pivots. We therefore propose RP-OPSD, Reasoning-Pivot-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation, using the distributional shift between matched teacher views with and without an English reference solution as an operational proxy to guide privileged distillation and reference anchoring. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks covering 17 languages and multiple difficulty levels show that our method outperforms strong multilingual reasoning baselines and OPSD variants. Further analysis reveals that RP-OPSD concentrates privileged distillation on reasoning-control and problem-condistioned state-update tokens, while downweighting it for tokens that mainly support surface realization. Our code is available at https://github.com/NJUNLP/RP-OPSD.

</details>


### [167] [The Bitter Lesson of Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06370)
*Ishan Patel,Sahil Sen,Elias Lumer,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性地评测了LLM模型中以代码方式进行工具调用（PTC），并首次在真实任务场景和标准基准集上比较了PTC与传统JSON方式的优劣。结果表明，PTC在模型能力持续提升的同时，具备更高的性能和更强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM成为智能体，可用工具扩展模型能力已成趋势。尤其是代码能力强的模型，程序化工具调用（PTC）推进了模型的灵活性与多样性。然而，缺乏对该方案在标准任务和多代模型上的系统性评估，因此本论文立项弥补这一空白。

Method: 作者在BFCL v4基准集上，针对14种LLM模型，细致比较了PTC与JSON工具调用。在PTC方案中，将工具以带类型的Python存根方式暴露，模型通过代码动态调用，整个过程在单次Agent回合内高效处理。同时还考察了在高并发及上下文变化场景下的表现。

Result: 实验证明，PTC在14个模型中有11个超过或持平于传统JSON工具，GPT-5.6系列相较基线提升10.6%。在并行调用场景下，有13个模型PTC表现更佳。在上下文扰动（context rot）下PTC依然稳定，而基线平均下降2.3%。

Conclusion: 程序化工具调用为LLM工具使用提供了更灵活、鲁棒的方式，可以很好地替代传统JSON方案，且随模型能力提升表现更佳。

Abstract: Tool use transforms LLMs into agents that act beyond their training data, and for code-capable models, programmatic tool calling extends this further by replacing rigid JSON calls with scripts that chain and parallelize naturally. However, a systematic evaluation of tools as code on an established benchmark across current and prior model generations under real-world task conditions has not been conducted. In this work, we empirically compare programmatic tool calling (PTC) to native JSON tool calling across 14 language models on BFCL v4. In the programmatic tool calling paradigm, tools are exposed as typed Python stubs that the model invokes through code, with execution and results handled in a single agent turn. Programmatic tool calling matches or exceeds native JSON tool calling in 11 of 14 models on BFCL v4, with the GPT-5.6 family achieving a 10.6% improvement over the JSON tool calling baseline. Further, it matches or outperforms baseline in 13 of 14 models under parallel fan-out, and holds stable under context rot conditions where baseline degrades 2.3% on average. Our results demonstrate that programmatic tool calling is a viable and robust alternative to JSON tool calling, with performance tracking model capability across release generations.

</details>


### [168] [Learning When to Trust via Selective Context Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06377)
*Xian Sun,Wei Chow,Yingshuo Wang,Junhao Liu,Wei Gao,Qing Wu,Lingdong Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个衡量语言模型对外部信号“选择性信任”能力的新基准与方法，指出仅增强模型抵抗误导性信号而忽视信任可靠信息会导致新的失效模式，并提出了新的衡量指标和优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型强化对误导信号的鲁棒性时，容易导致模型直接忽略所有上下文信息，这使得模型在面对可靠上下文时反而表现较差。因此，需要更细致地研究模型何时应“信任”或“怀疑”上下文。

Method: 提出MIST数据集，对每个推理题目设计四种上下文条件（干净、误导、正确信息和无关信息），并定义了SC2W指标来度量模型被误导信息诱导出错的频率。进一步提出SCOPE优化框架，结合直接偏好优化（DPO）目标，对四类条件中平衡配对的样本进行训练。

Result: 基于MIST基准的实验显示，大多数流行的开源模型对误导上下文一律易受影响。所提出的SCOPE方法在有效降低SC2W的同时，能够保持模型在信任值得信息和无关信息上的高准确性。

Conclusion: 作者强调模型评估应侧重于“选择性信任”能力而非简单的抵抗误导。SCOPE和MIST为促进该方向研究提供了标准化工具与评估方法。

Abstract: Language models increasingly condition their answers on external signals, and a single misleading one can turn a correct answer wrong. The obvious remedy, training models to resist such signals, hides a failure mode: a model that ignores all context looks robust yet is useless when the context is worth trusting. We recast the problem as selective trust and introduce MIST, a human-annotated benchmark that renders each reasoning item under four matched conditions (clean, misleading, correct-context, and irrelevant-context), together with SC2W, a paired metric counting how often a misleading signal flips a clean-correct answer to wrong. Across a comprehensive benchmark study, we observe that such a susceptibility is universal. We then propose SCOPE, which mines clean-correct/misleading-wrong failures and optimizes a standard Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) objective over matched preference pairs balanced equally across all four conditions, rather than over misleading items alone. Our approach substantially reduces SC2W on popular open-sourced models while preserving accuracy when the added context is clean, correct, or irrelevant. With this work, we argue that models should be judged on selective trust, not on resistance alone.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [VLAff: Vision-Language-Affordance Model for Unified Actionable Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05215)
*Jihoon Oh,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从人类视频中学习可操作性能力的新框架，通过3D重建和手部网格恢复技术，提取与动作无关的可操作性信息，并创建了大规模数据集和统一视觉语言基础模型，在利用视觉观察和指令生成可直接执行的机器人动作方面表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用人类视频进行机器人操作技能学习具有很大潜能，但由于人机体现差异，这一过程存在挑战。学习与体现无关的物体可操作性（affordance）被认为是一种有前途的解决思路。

Method: 提出利用第一视角人类视频，通过3D结构光流与手部网格重建技术，提取可操作性信息（包括视觉、抓取和运动轨迹）。构建了大规模EgoAffordance数据集，并开发基于视觉-语言大模型的统一模型VLAff，实现多模态可操作性学习及可直接转化为机器人动作的操作指令。

Result: VLAff模型在视觉可操作性预测任务上取得了最先进的性能，并能有效应用于真实机器人，如零样本（zero-shot）操作和可操作性引导的机器人学习。

Conclusion: 本文方法能够有效缓解人机体现差异对机器人技能迁移的影响，通过大数据与统一模型促进了可操作性学习，在实际机器人自动执行操作任务中具有良好应用前景。

Abstract: Learning manipulation skills from human videos is promising for scalable robot learning. However, the embodiment mismatch between humans and robots makes this challenging. One promising solution is to learn object-centric actionable affordances that are embodiment-agnostic. In this work, we propose a framework that leverages egocentric human videos with state-of-the-art 3D Structure-from-Motion and hand mesh reconstruction to extract actionable affordances such as visual, grasp, and trajectory affordances that explicitly encode where to interact, how to grasp, and how to move. We construct EgoAffordance, a large-scale dataset comprising 204K episodes with 5.6M visual affordances and 11.6M grasp and trajectory affordances. Building on this, we introduce VLAff, a large vision-language model-based unified foundation model that learns cross-modal correlations across all actionable affordances. Given a visual observation and instruction, VLAff generates visual affordance heatmaps, grasp poses, and trajectories, which are then converted into directly executable actions by utilizing 3D scene information. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that VLAff not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on visual affordance prediction, but can also be effectively applied to real robot applications such as zero-shot manipulation and affordance-guided robot learning.

</details>


### [170] [A System for Train Condition Monitoring and Structural Health Assessment of Rail Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05221)
*Maximilian Posner,Martin Dazer,Daniela Lauer,Robert Winkler-Höhn,Mathilde Laporte,Tobias Herrmann,Martin Köppel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合结构传感技术与AI数据分析的轨道车辆实时状态监测与冲击检测方案，验证了在提升运营安全与实现预测性维护方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前主线铁路在实现全自动化（GoA4）方面受限于安全要求与环境复杂性，现有感知系统难以精准识别撞击和碾压等事件，亟需新的监测与检测手段。

Method: 整合结构传感器与AI算法，搭建了一个实时监测平台，能够自动检测和识别影响轨道车辆运行安全的关键事件并进行数据分析。该平台聚焦于冲击、损伤及碾压事件检测，连续健康监控以辅助维护，以及为车辆设计优化提供长期数据分析。

Result: 实验结果验证了该方案的可行性，系统能够实时有效识别多类事件，并为维护、设计等提供决策支持数据。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅提升了主线铁路运营安全和维护效率，也为未来实现全面自动化运行提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: The ongoing digitalization of rail systems and the increasing use of artificial intelligence (AI) are fundamentally transforming the design, operation, and maintenance of rail vehicles. While fully automated operation at Grade of Automation 4 (GoA4) is well established in metro systems, its deployment in mainline rail remains limited. This is primarily due to stringent safety requirements and the complexity of open operational environments. Current perception systems based on cameras, radar, and lidar are effective in detecting objects but provide limited capability for reliably identifying impacts, collisions, and driving-over events. This paper presents a novel approach for real-time vehicle condition monitoring and impact detection that integrates structural sensor technologies with AI-based data analysis. The proposed framework addresses three key applications: (1) automated detection of impacts, structural damage, and driving-over events, (2) condition-based maintenance enabled by continuous monitoring, and (3) long-term data analytics to support vehicle design optimization. The results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed approach and highlight its potential to enhance operational safety, enable predictive maintenance strategies, and support the transition toward fully automated operation in mainline rail systems

</details>


### [171] [Failing Gracefully: Mitigating Impact of Inevitable Robot Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05313)
*Duc M. Nguyen,Saad A. Ghani,Andrew Marshall,Allison Andreyev,Gregory J. Stein,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的安全评估方法，用于量化服务机器人在家庭环境中因故障可能对周围实体造成的影响，并开发了故障仿真平台FailBench进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在家庭环境中会遇到不可避免的故障，这些故障可能带来安全风险。因此，亟需一种系统化方法来评估和降低故障带来的负面影响，提升实际部署的可靠性和安全性。

Method: 作者提出了一种安全公式，可以同时评估机器人故障时与环境中各实体（例如人、宠物、物品）产生影响性互动的概率和后果严重程度。方法通过量化不同实体受故障影响的风险，指导机器人做出兼顾安全与效率的规划决策。此外，作者还提出了FailBench——一个基于MuJoCo的仿真平台，可以系统性地复现和研究各种故障模式下的机器人-环境交互。

Result: 实验通过FailBench对多种服务机器人故障场景进行仿真和评估，结果表明所提安全评估公式能有效区分不同交互和结果的风险，为安全性优化提供可靠依据。

Conclusion: 所提出的安全评估方法和FailBench平台为服务机器人在家庭环境中的安全性研究和部署提供了理论基础和实验工具，有助于开发更安全、更健壮的移动规划与策略。

Abstract: Service robots operate in household environments shared with humans, pets, and everyday objects, where they are highly susceptible to failures such as software crashes, hardware degradation, or unpredictable interactions. While roboticists strive to minimize failures, some remain inevitable, making it critical to mitigate their potential consequences for safe and reliable deployment. This paper introduces a novel safety formulation that evaluates both the probability of impactful interactions between robots and surrounding entities during failures, and the severity of their outcomes. By quantifying the impact of failures on different entities, our approach enables robots to make informed planning decisions that balance safety with task efficiency. To support systematic evaluation, we also present FailBench, a MuJoCo-based simulation framework for studying robot-environment interactions under diverse failure modes, including sensing issues and actuator malfunctions. Together, our safety formulation and FailBench provide a foundation for developing safer and more robust motion plans and learned policies in real-world household environments.

</details>


### [172] [Unified Planning-Learning Framework for Robust UUV Navigation Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05365)
*Md Ether Deowan,Eleni Kelasidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种专为无人水下航行器（UUV）在动态水下环境中设计的观测型自主导航框架，结合了持续占据映射、清晰度感知规划与风险感知局部控制，通过多传感器融合和强化学习提升鲁棒性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态和部分可观测的水下环境中，UUV自动导航极具挑战性，现有方法难以兼顾长短时规划与不确定性感知。本文旨在提升UUV面对复杂变化场景下的自主能力和安全性。

Method: 框架包括：1）仅利用机载声呐和深度摄像头的环境占据建图；2）集成了考虑避障裕度的全局规划器提供长时结构指导；3）将强化学习策略与局部轨迹跟踪及反应式避障结合；4）引入了从传感器数据学习的潜在状态表示增强决策；5）行为树蒸馏及分阶段监督，用校准机制提升训练稳定性与安全。

Result: 通过在NVIDIA Isaac Sim高保真模拟环境中的多种随机种子验证协议，和行为树基线、标准RL基线对比，所提方法在动态环境下显著提升了鲁棒性和安全性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的混合规划学习框架为UUV在部分可观测、动态水下环境中的自主导航提供了统一、可复现的解决方案，并验证了其实用性与优势。

Abstract: This paper presents an observation-only autonomy framework for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles (UUVs) navigation in dynamic underwater environments that integrates persistent occupancy mapping, global clearance-aware planning, and risk-aware local control. The proposed pipeline constructs occupancy maps solely from onboard sonar and depth image observations, adapts a clearance-constrained global planner (GP) to provide long-horizon structure, and integrates a reinforcement learning (RL) policy to handle short-range tracking and reactive avoidance. To further support decision-making under partial observability, the system learns a compact latent state representation from onboard sensor data, encoding environmental structure, obstacle dynamics, and uncertainty. Behavior tree (BT) distillation with staged supervision is introduced to improve safety and training stability, while an uncertainty-calibrated distillation mechanism reweights teacher guidance using online latent-model uncertainty, emphasizing uncertain regimes during learning, with time-to-collision (TTC) and clearance cues remaining explicit in planning and local policy features. To demonstrate the efficacy of the framework, a reproducible multi-seed evaluation protocol is established in high-fidelity GPU-accelerated simulation using NVIDIA Isaac Sim, and performance is benchmarked against BT-only and standard RL baselines. The results obtained demonstrate improved robustness and safety under dynamic conditions, thus providing a general pipeline with a unified hybrid planning learning architecture and a reproducible methodology for robust UUV autonomy under partial observability.

</details>


### [173] [World-to-Wrist: Task-Conditioned Future Wrist Modeling for Fine-Grained Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05369)
*Yuhao Pan,Haosong Peng,Zhengshen Zhang,Zhengyang Yan,Yalun Dai,Fushuo Huo,Chujie Wang,Tianyu Qi,Xiucheng Wang,Nan Cheng,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型W2-VLA，利用任务条件预测未来手腕局部状态，提升了机器人细粒度操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将主视角与手腕视角等同对待，未能挖掘手腕视角预见与全局任务语境的结合对精细操作的重要性，影响了机器人在复杂操作任务中的表现。

Method: 作者提出W2-VLA，通过多视角观测和任务指令，构建紧凑的潜变量接口，连接视觉-语言和手腕预测模块。手腕预测器利用该接口和手腕历史信息，预测未来手腕状态，并反馈至动作决策。此外，提出W2-CoT自动合成标注流程，对操作进展、物理变化等进行结构化标注，为潜变量接口提供辅助监督。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真实机器人操作任务上，W2-VLA展现出更优的细粒度和接触敏感操作表现，适用于单臂和双臂场景，且动作生成速率超过80Hz。

Conclusion: W2-VLA通过任务条件下手腕未来状态建模，有效提升了视觉-语言-动作一体化模型在机器人精细操作中的表现，具备良好的泛化性和实时性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often treat main-view and wrist-view observations as parallel visual inputs, overlooking their distinct roles in robot manipulation. Fine-grained manipulation, however, benefits from anticipating how wrist-local interactions may evolve under the global task context. To address this limitation, we present World-to-Wrist VLA (W2-VLA), a VLA model for fine-grained robot manipulation with task-conditioned future wrist modeling. Given current multi-view observations and a task instruction, W2-VLA contextualizes a set of latent modeling tokens as a compact interface between the vision-language model and the wrist predictor. Conditioned on this interface and the observed wrist history, the predictor forecasts future wrist latents, which are transformed into future-aware context for action prediction. In addition, we introduce W2-CoT, a synthesis pipeline that produces structured annotations describing manipulation progress, physical transition cues, and wrist-local evidence. These annotations provide auxiliary supervision that shapes the task-conditioned latent interface. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved fine-grained and contact-sensitive manipulation across both single-arm and bimanual settings, while maintaining action-generation rates above 80 Hz.

</details>


### [174] [Sliding Sensors: Configurable Confidence in State Estimation for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05410)
*Ella Walsh,Spencer Teetaert,Eric Diller,Timothy D. Barfoot,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过机械可重构的传感器，实现对连续体机器人状态估计不确定性的定向调控（uncertainty-shaping）的方法。作者展示了通过在机器人内部纵向移动传感器位置，可以提升与任务相关部位的估计信心，并显著降低整机形状估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人常常在不确定的环境中工作，状态估计的准确性对安全至关重要，但测量不确定性在空间上分布不均。实际任务中，更需要在关键位置获得高估计信心，而非全局准确。为此，需要研究如何对估计不确定性进行有效调控。

Method: 作者提出一种可机械重构的传感器布局，即传感器能沿纵向在机器人内部移动，并设计了相关的硬件原理样机。通过变换传感器位置，动态调整测量点，提高了在任务相关部位的估计信心。

Result: 实验结果显示，能够通过移动传感器位置来调整状态估计的不确定性。与固定在端部的单一传感器相比，滑动传感器能显著降低对整机形状的估计误差。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过机械重构的传感器布置，可有效调控连续体机器人状态估计的不确定性，提升关键部位的估计信心，对实际应用安全性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Continuum robots often operate in uncertain environments, where accurate state estimation is essential for safe interactions. Estimate uncertainty is inherently spatially non-uniform: confidence varies depending on where measurements are available. Global estimation accuracy is not always the top priority, but rather achieving sufficient confidence at task-relevant locations along the robot. This extended abstract introduces mechanically reconfigurable sensing enabling uncertainty-shaping in state estimation for continuum robots. We present a concept hardware design demonstrating the feasibility of longitudinal translation of a sensor within a continuum robot. We demonstrate that state estimation confidence can be reconfigured by varying the sensor location, and show a reduction of full-body shape estimation errors when sliding the sensor back and forth over time, compared to a single fixed tip sensor.

</details>


### [175] [ARGUS: Aligning Robot Scene Geometry Under Shifting Views with Large 3D Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05579)
*Rishik Sathua,Haonan Chen,Katherine Driggs-Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的观测预处理流程ARGUS，通过使用大规模3D视觉模型，将任意摄像头视角的图像预先对齐到标准视角，从而提升机器人操作策略在多样视角数据集上的泛化和学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人操控视觉运动策略在多视角任务中往往会将场景几何与视角绑定，难以从多视角数据中学习并泛化超出训练时的视角分布。

Method: 引入ARGUS预处理流程，将原始图像通过3D视觉模型统一对齐到规范视角，再交由后续的视觉运动策略模块处理。通过与多种训练数据的比较实验评测效果。

Result: ARGUS在从不同视角和不同摄像机布置采集的数据上，均超越了已有方法，在有限视角和多样视角下训练效果更好，并能以4-6倍于前人方法的速度收敛到高成功率。

Conclusion: 利用大规模3D视觉模型进行观测标准化，有效减轻了视觉运动策略的学习负担，实现了对大规模、多样视角机器人数据的更高效学习。

Abstract: Large-scale visuomotor policies have demonstrated impressive performance across a wide range of robot manipulation tasks. However, despite this success, manipulation polices often entangle scene geometry with the corresponding viewpoint, learning where objects lie in an image rather than where it lies in the task space. This entanglement inherently limits the corresponding policy's ability to learn from viewpoint-diverse datasets (ex. DROID, BridgeV2) and generalize beyond the viewpoints captured in their training data. In this work, we present ARGUS, an observation pre-processing pipeline that uses large-scale 3D vision models to align image observations from arbitrary camera viewpoints into a canonical viewpoint before passing it to downstream visuomotor policies. Experiments across training datasets with varying levels of viewpoint diversity, from fixed multi-view camera configurations to highly varied camera placements, show that our method consistently outperforms prior approaches across both limited-view and view-diverse training regimes. In efficiency comparisons, ARGUS demonstrates an ability to learn from view-diverse data, converging to high success rates 4-6x faster than previous methods by leveraging a simplified observation space. Overall, our findings show that leveraging large-scale 3D vision models reduces the learning burden on visuomotor policies, enabling more efficient learning from large-scale, viewpoint-diverse robot datasets.

</details>


### [176] [PathCover: A Fast Convex Decomposition along a Path via Randomized Iterative Space Partitioning (RISP) on Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05586)
*Kunal S. Narkhede,Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Guoquan Huang,Ioannis Poulakakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PathCover框架，实现了基于LiDAR原始点云的高效凸多面体走廊生成，显著提升机器人实时导航能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人导航走廊生成器难以满足实时、传感器速率的运算需求，急需更快的算法以适应实际环境。

Method: 提出PathCover框架，核心为随机化RISP算法，能在预期线性时间内直接从点云生成凸多面体，生成重叠无障碍多面体序列供后续轨迹优化。算法设计保证在有限步骤内终止，并确保沿无障碍参考路径连续前进。

Result: 在合成和真实LiDAR数据集上的基准测试表明，该方法较当前最佳方案实现了数量级的速度提升，并保持了相近的走廊体积。

Conclusion: PathCover框架有效支撑了四旋翼仿真和四足机器人实地导航实验，实现了高效、安全的导航走廊生成，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Autonomous robot navigation requires the rapid generation of obstacle-free regions for trajectory planning. However, existing corridor generators struggle to meet real-time, sensor-rate computational constraints. To resolve this bottleneck, we introduce PathCover, a framework driven by RISP; a novel randomized algorithm that constructs convex polytopes directly from raw point cloud data in expected linear time under a mild probabilistic elimination condition. PathCover generates sequences of overlapping, obstacle-free polytopes that safely constrain downstream MPC and trajectory optimization. We mathematically guarantee that the algorithm terminates in finite steps while ensuring continuous progress along any obstacle-free reference path. Extensive benchmarks on synthetic and real-world LiDAR datasets demonstrate an order-of-magnitude speedup over state-of-the-art methods while maintaining comparable corridor volumes. The complete pipeline is validated via high-fidelity quadrotor simulations and physical deployment on a quadrupedal robot navigating constrained environments using live LiDAR perception.

</details>


### [177] [Search-Aided Joint Agent-Environment Reinforcement Learning for Robust Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding with Rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05588)
*He Jiang,Jingtian Yan,Yulun Zhang,Yimin Tang,Tanishq Duhan,Rishi Veerapaneni,Guillaume Sartoretti,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对现实自动化仓库系统中的多智能体路径规划（LMAPF-R2）问题，提出了一种新的联合强化学习方法（SJRL），实现了在高密度、运动约束更复杂环境下大规模机器人协作路径规划的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LMAPF算法在运动学假设上过于简单，无法满足现实场景下如自动化仓库的安全性和转向等复杂约束，因此需要更贴合实际应用的多智能体路径规划模型与方法。

Method: 提出了更贴近真实系统的LMAPF-R2模型，引入安全约束与原地旋转约束，并设计了Search-Aided Joint Reinforcement Learning（SJRL）方法：1）将Causal PIBT搜索规划与神经策略结合，实现单步碰撞解决与意图传播；2）环境策略通过学习图边权并结合反向Dijkstra搜索，为全局移动提供指导，实现环境与智能体的联合优化。

Result: 在多个高密度图环境中，SJRL方法相较于强大的搜索类规划器Causal PIBT表现出显著性能提升。此外，在现实与虚拟混合的仓库场景（8台实体机器人与248个虚拟机器人协作）中验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: SJRL能够有效提升具复杂运动约束多智能体系统的路径规划性能，对自动化仓库等实际应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding (LMAPF) requires repeatedly planning collision-free paths for agents that continuously receive new goals upon reaching their current ones. While many learning-based planners have been proposed for LMAPF, most rely on oversimplified kinematic assumptions that may overlook motion constraints critical to real-world performance. In this work, we study a more realistic LMAPF model derived from many real-world automated warehouse systems, termed LMAPF-R2, which incorporates robust safety constraints and in-place rotation constraints. These constraints substantially increase coordination difficulty, particularly in highly constrained spaces. To address these challenges, we propose Search-Aided Joint Reinforcement Learning (SJRL). We first augment neural policies with Causal PIBT, a single-step search-based planner that resolves agents' collisions and propagates their intentions. We then introduce a unified RL formulation that jointly optimizes agent and environment policies, where the environment policy learns graph edge costs to provide global movement guidance via backward Dijkstra search. Experiments demonstrate that SJRL achieves significant improvements over the strong search-based planner, Causal-PIBT, across multiple high-density maps. We further validate SJRL in a challenging mixed-reality warehouse environment with 8 physical robots and 248 virtual robots.

</details>


### [178] [KILVO: Kinematic-Inertial-LiDAR-Visual Odometry with Robust Multimodal Adaptation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05647)
*Jixin Gao,Fucheng Liu,Teng Zhang,Fusheng Zha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种针对人形机器人设计的多模态融合定位系统KILVO，集成了运动学、惯性、激光雷达和视觉信息，实现了更精准和鲁棒的状态估计，并展示出优秀实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人状态估计算法对于实际环境复杂性和多传感器融合利用不充分，且在传感器失效时鲁棒性较弱，需要开发适用性更强的状态估计方法。

Method: 基于异步-顺序混合的误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波（ESIKF）框架，将关节编码器、IMU、LiDAR和摄像头数据有效结合。采用惯性数据进行预测，高速异步处理腿部运动学信息以提供本体约束，外部感知（激光雷达和视觉）则顺序更新用于几何和光度误差校正。此外，引入多模态自适应机制增强对传感器失效的鲁棒性，并设计了紧凑的触地检测模块无需额外传感器。

Result: KILVO在公共数据集和多种人形机器人、步态、场景的真实实验中，显示了高准确性、高效率及高输出速率，对传感器退化和故障具备显著鲁棒性，整体性能优于现有主流融合算法。

Conclusion: KILVO更适合于实际的人形机器人应用，提升了状态估计精度和鲁棒性，为复杂环境下的机器人运动提供了有力支持，相关代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: This article presents a kinematic-inertial-LiDAR-visual odometry for humanoid robots, called KILVO. Tailored to the platform features, requirements, and real-world complexity, it fully utilizes the sensors commonly equipped on humanoid robots, including joint encoders, IMU, LiDAR, and camera, within an asynchronous-sequential hybrid error-state iterated Kalman filter (ESIKF). Specifically, inertial data are used for prediction, leg kinematics are processed asynchronously at a high rate and provide proprioceptive constraints, while exteroception is updated sequentially, first by registering LiDAR points for geometric priors and then by updating the visual component via photometric errors. Moreover, the framework is elaborately designed with multimodal adaptation for resilience to sensor failures. A compact contact estimation module is also developed, sharing information with state estimation without additional sensors. Extensive experiments on public datasets and in the real world across multiple humanoid robots, gait patterns, and scenarios demonstrate that KILVO achieves highly competitive accuracy, efficiency, and output rates, with strong robustness against sensor degradation and failures, making it more suitable for humanoid robots than state-of-the-art fusion methods. Our code and datasets are released on GitHub.

</details>


### [179] [JoyAI-RA 0.5: Scaling Robot Manipulation Learning via Dual Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05674)
*JoyAI-RA Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了JoyAI-RA 0.5框架，通过引入显式和隐式动作对齐，解决了人类视角、仿真与机器人数据之间的异构问题，大幅提升了机器人泛化策略对不同数据源的适应能力，实现了高效的跨模态抓取与操作任务学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人数据有限，难以单靠机器人自身数据实现泛化和复杂能力学习，人类视频数据量大但与机器人差异较大，简单合并反而导致知识负迁移。因此，需要找到有效的方法，整合多源、异构数据，提升机器人操作范围和学习效率。

Method: 提出了通用的Vision-Language-World-Action (VLWA)结构JoyAI-RA 0.5，结合物理世界动力学先验与视觉语义信息。设计了两类动作对齐机制：隐式动作对齐通过视觉转移推断潜在动作，实现对动作标签缺失的数据指导学习；显式对齐将人类与机器人轨迹统一到同一物理动作空间，并引入相机帧+块相对末端执行器动作表达。最后采用内-外环式强化学习进行高效适应和策略优化。

Result: 在实际AgiBot平台测试表明，JoyAI-RA在已见和未见任务上均表现出色，尤其当人类视角数据规模扩大时任务分数持续提升，并未出现饱和，验证了弱标注人类视频数据可高效转化为机器人训练信号。

Conclusion: 海量人类视频数据不仅可以作为辅助信息，更能成为拓展机器人操作能力的主要路径。JoyAI-RA展现了跨模态、多源数据整合下提升机器人泛化和实际表现的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Robot data is scarce, so generalist policies need to learn from heterogeneous sources, including human egocentric video, simulation, and real robots, which differ in supervision and embodiment, with action labels missing or mutually incompatible. Human egocentric data scale best but sit farthest from robot data, and naive pooling causes negative transfer rather than knowledge sharing. We propose JoyAI-RA 0.5, a generalist Vision-Language-World-Action (VLWA) framework that couples physical world-dynamics priors with visual semantics and scales manipulation learning across such data via dual action alignment. Implicit action alignment infers latent actions from visual transitions, enabling action-free human, simulation, and robot data to guide a latent-action-conditioned world model in learning physical dynamics. Explicit alignment grounds reliable human and robot trajectories in a unified physical action space through a canonical action representation and camera-frame chunk-relative end-effector actions. An inner-outer-loop reinforcement stage then pairs efficient task adaptation with foundation-policy improvement. On a real-world AgiBot benchmark, JoyAI-RA performs strongly on both seen tasks and unseen variations. The task score improves consistently as the volume of human egocentric pretraining data increases and shows no sign of plateauing at our largest scale. This suggests that abundant but weakly labeled human experience can be converted into a transferable training signal, making human video not merely a weak auxiliary source but a primary axis along which manipulation capability can be scaled. Project page can be found at https://joyai-ra-05.github.io/.

</details>


### [180] [Nonvisual Classification of Ground-Condition by Artificial Proprioception in an Amoeba-Inspired Autonomous Walking Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05684)
*Hyoto Yamaguchi,Zenji Yatabe,Seiya Kasai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态传感的地面状态无视觉分类方法，并应用于模拟变形虫行走机器人的自主步态调整。该方法无需图像传感，仅利用加速度计、压力传感器和水库计算实现高精度地面识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多自主机器人依赖视觉传感识别地面状况，但图像系统受环境光线、遮挡等影响，且运算复杂。为提升自适应性和简化硬件，研究希望实现无需视觉即可高效分类地面条件。

Method: 将带有三轴加速度计和八足底压力传感器的四足仿生机器人平台作为实验对象，并采用水库计算（reservoir computing）处理传感数据，开发人工本体感觉（proprioception）系统，实现对平坦和崎岖地面的高准确分类。

Result: 即使机器人的动态步态导致传感器信号大幅波动，该系统仍能准确区分平坦与崎岖地面，实现地面条件的高效识别。同时，实测中实现了步态与地面状态的实时切换。

Conclusion: 多模态传感联合水库计算可在无视觉条件下实现高精度地面分类，提高了仿生行走机器人对环境的适应性。并进一步分析和讨论了各传感器在分类中的作用。

Abstract: Nonvisual classification of ground condition based on a multimodal sensing approach was investigated for an amoeba-inspired autonomous walking robot. To classify ground condition without image sensing and processing, we implemented artificial proprioception by integrating a three-axis accelerometer, eight foot pressure sensors, and reservoir computing (RC). Even when large fluctuations in the sensor outputs are caused by dynamic motions of a four-legged robot in walking, our system can classify the ground condition, flat or rough, with high accuracy. We demonstrate on-site switching of walking gait depending on ground condition in the robot. We also discuss the contribution of each sensor to ground condition classification.

</details>


### [181] [Hijacking Robots with a Piece of Paper: A Systematic Study of Physical Prompt Injection in VLM-Controlled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05715)
*S. M . Bhagya P. Samarakoon,M. A. Viraj J. Muthugala,W. K. R. Sachinthana,Mohan Rajesh Elara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文系统性研究了针对视觉-语言模型（VLM）在机器人系统中作为规划器时物理提示注入攻击的问题，包括提出攻击分类法和基准测试，并分析了现有VLM的易受攻击性及防御措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在机器人任务中广泛应用，但其结合视觉与指令跟随的特性引入了自然而新的攻击面，特别是现实环境中人类可读的物理文本可能被模型解析为有效提示，导致行为偏转。因此，研究相关攻击类型、防御和影响具有重要意义。

Method: 作者提出了四类物理提示攻击（间接标识、任务重定义、权威冒充、冲突注入），设计了20个攻击提示，结合三种场景布局和三类命令表述，在GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen3-VL-32B三种主流VLM下共进行5670次实验。同时，对模型推理过程进行了溯源分析，并评估了三种简单防御策略（提示级防御、两阶段验证、文本掩码）。

Result: 结果显示，三种模型的攻击成功率分别为27.0%、29.4%和5.0%；其中权威冒充和否定类攻击在三个模型间具有迁移性。绝大多数攻击被模型意识到并承认。不同模型采用了不同防御机制。三种防御措施在不同模型上有效性为75%-100%、85%-100%和100%。

Conclusion: VLM驱动的机器人操作对物理世界人类可读标识高度敏感且存在显著安全风险，但通过简单的防御手段可显著降低风险。防御策略需权衡安全与任务完成能力，尤其对需要视觉识别文本的任务存在一定影响。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as planners in robotic systems, where they translate natural-language commands into executable actions grounded in visual scene understanding. This tight coupling between perception and instruction-following introduces a new attack surface: adversarial text placed within the robot's visual field can act as an indirect prompt injection into the VLM's reasoning stack. We present a systematic study of physical prompt injection attacks against VLM-controlled sorting, introducing a four-category taxonomy, indirect signage, task redefinition, authority impersonation, and conflict injection, instantiated as a benchmark of 20 attack prompts evaluated across three physical scene layouts and three command formulations that vary in destination specificity and rule explicitness. Across 5,670 trials on three frontier VLMs (GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen3-VL-32B), attacks succeed at 27.0%, 29.4%, and 5.0% respectively, with authority-impersonating and negation attacks transferring across all three models. Analysis of reasoning traces reveals that successful compromise is nearly always conscious (99.9% acknowledgment rate), and that models defend through structurally different mechanisms, explicit rejection for Gemini, perceptual inattention for GPT-4o. We evaluate three simple mitigations: prompt-based defense (75-100% effective, model-dependent), two-stage verification (85-100%), and pre-processing text masking (100%). Our findings show that VLM-controlled manipulation is meaningfully vulnerable to human-readable physical signage, and that simple defenses substantially reduce risk, though defense choice involves trade-offs. The defenses preserve general task capabilities in our benchmark, but they may impair tasks that require reading in-scene labels.

</details>


### [182] [ATP: Anatomical Torque with Passivity-based Control Framework for Safe Upper-Limb Exoskeleton Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05723)
*Yu Chen,Gong Chen,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于解剖学的上肢外骨骼助力控制方法（ATP），结合肌肉动力学仿真、强化学习和被动性控制，实现了对复杂非周期性上肢动作的安全、通用辅助，显著降低了肌肉负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外骨骼系统主要针对下肢周期性动作，易于实现高精度助力；但上肢动作复杂多变，难以实现通用且安全的助力控制，对上肢解剖学助力研究不足。亟需可泛化、多任务适应、安全可靠的上肢外骨骼助力机制。

Method: 1）采用可扩展的肌肉骨骼仿真平台，训练统一的强化学习肌肉控制器，实现跨上肢动作的解剖参考力矩生成；2）在线力矩校正方案，适应多动作，抑制肌腱峰值力，结合异常分数保障安全与舒适；3）基于力矩的外骨骼助力控制器，通过缆索驱动且保持被动性，允许自由动作，不约束运动轨迹，同时利用能量池管理保障系统安全与跟踪精度。

Result: 仿真与真实实验均显示，该方法可精准跟踪长时间运动序列，具备良好实时性与动作泛化能力。能量池机制下可保证助力被动性和跟踪精度。肌电实验表明，相比重力补偿与开环助力，外骨骼可使目标肌群活动降低最多48%。

Conclusion: ATP助力方法实现了对上肢复杂动作的安全、舒适、精准外骨骼助力，具备较好泛化能力和被动性保障，有望推广至多任务和真实环境中的上肢外骨骼辅助领域。

Abstract: Providing assistance across diverse movements is a central objective of exoskeletons, and anatomical knowledge can enable responsive support that generalizes across tasks. However, anatomical assistance has mainly been studied for lower-limb exoskeletons, where periodic, weight-bearing motions impose lower demands on torque precision. Extending such assistance to complex, nonperiodic upper-limb movements remains challenging. This paper proposes Anatomical Torque with Passivity-Based Control (ATP) for safe upper-limb exoskeleton assistance. First, a scalable musculoskeletal simulation framework trains a unified reinforcement-learning muscle controller that generalizes across upper-limb movements and generates anatomical reference torques without complex biomechanical computations. Second, an online torque-refinement scheme adapts the reference to diverse movements, suppresses tendon-induced spikes, and incorporates a learned anomaly score for safe and comfortable assistance. Third, an interaction torque controller delivers assistance through a cable-driven compliant exoskeleton without constraining motion to predefined trajectories, while an energy tank preserves passivity with theoretical guarantees on torque tracking and system passivity. Simulations and real-world experiments show accurate tracking of long-duration motion sequences and generalization to real-time human movements. The controller achieves accurate torque tracking while preserving passivity and resumes tracking after energy-tank replenishment. An EMG study with five participants further shows reduced target-muscle activity during static and dynamic tasks compared with gravity compensation and open-loop assistance, with reductions of up to 48% relative to movement without the exoskeleton in a dynamic multi-joint task.

</details>


### [183] [Near-sensor Computing for Rapid Visuotactile Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05725)
*Zhengying Zhu,Ruilin Zhang,Runze Hu,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO
